  .STATE -- (id=706871296, тип=Управляющие каналы линии 1) -- 
      | local s;
      | local snl, ref;
      | 
      | if (self.allocate='C')
      | 
      |   if (self.state = -1000)
      |     self.Repair := 1;
      |   else
      |     self.Repair := 0;
      |   end; 
      |   
      |   self.State_str := prj.GetStateStr(self.State);
      |   ref:= self.R('S1');
      |   if (self.priority>1)
      |     
      |     if (self.State<-1)
      |       s:=alltrim(str(-1*self.State));
      |       snl := alltrim(str(self.line));
      |       ref.PlayOnce('l'+snl, '_'+s);
      |     else
      |       if (self.State=-1)
      |         ref.PlayOnce('stop');
      |       else
      |         if (self.State=0)
      |           ref.PlayOnce('finish');
      |         end;
      |       end;
      |     end;
      |     
      |     
      |   end;
      |   
      | end;
      | 
      | if (self.allocate='S')
      |   local cmd, PStat;
      |   
      |   cmd := 'PStat' + STR( self.Line, 1 );
      |   PStat := &&cmd;  
      |   
      |   if (self.state < -1)
      |     SIRENA.Light:=1;
      |     PStat.str_errors := PStat.str_errors + ALLTRIM( STR( ABS( self.state ), 3 ) + ',' ); 
      |     PStat.step_errors := PStat.step_errors + ALLTRIM( STR( ABS( self.state ), 3 ) + ',' );
      |   end;
      |   
      | end;

  .CMD -- (id=706871298, тип=Управляющие каналы линии 1) -- 
      | function StatePar(self);
      |   if (self.Allocate = 'C')
      |     if ((self.ST=0) and (self.ERR=0) )
      |       self.selframe(1);
      |     end;
      |     if ((self.ST=1) and (self.ERR=0) )
      |       self.selframe(2);
      |     end;
      |     if ((self.ERR>0) )
      |       self.selframe(3);
      |     end;
      |   end;
      | endf;

  .RT_PAR_F[1] (id=705298432, тип=Параметры линии 1) -- Расход концентрата при наведении
      | if (self.allocate='C') 
      | 
      | if (self.State=-2)   
      |   self.selframe(6);
      | end;
      | 
      | if (self.State=-1)   
      |   self.selframe(5);
      | end;
      | 
      | if (self.State=1)
      |    self.selframe(2);
      |  {self.animate:=true;
      | else
      |  self.animate:=false;} 
      | end;
      | 
      | if (self.State=0)   
      |   self.selframe(1);
      | end;
      | 
      | end;

      |     else
      |       if (self.State=-1)

      |       else
